function renderEquip(){
  [&#x27;weapon&#x27;,&#x27;helmet&#x27;,&#x27;armor&#x27;,&#x27;gloves&#x27;,&#x27;ring&#x27;,&#x27;legs&#x27;,&#x27;boots&#x27;,&#x27;necklace&#x27;,&#x27;artifact&#x27;,&#x27;cloak&#x27;,&#x27;wings&#x27;,&#x27;pet&#x27;].forEach(function(s){
    var el=document.getElementById(&#x27;eq_&#x27;+s);if(!el)return;
    var it=_inv.equipped[s],rc=it?(RCOL[it.rarity]||&#x27;#a26620&#x27;):&#x27;#a26620&#x27;;
    if(it){
      el.style.borderColor=rc;
      el.style.background=rarityBg(it.rarity);
      el.innerHTML=&#x27;&lt;div style=&quot;width:100%;height:calc(100% - 15px);display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;overflow:hidden&quot;&gt;&#x27;+itemVisual(it,&#x27;fill&#x27;)+&#x27;&lt;/div&gt;&#x27;+enhBadge(it)+&#x27;&lt;span class=&quot;eqLabel&quot; style=&quot;color:#f0d7a2;text-shadow:0 1px 2px #000&quot;&gt;&#x27;+SLOTLBL[s]+&#x27;&lt;/span&gt;&#x27;;
    }else{
      el.style.borderColor=&#x27;&#x27;;
      el.style.background=&#x27;&#x27;;
      el.innerHTML=&#x27;&lt;span class=&quot;eqLabel&quot;&gt;&#x27;+SLOTLBL[s]+&#x27;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span class=&quot;eqFree&quot;&gt;свободно&lt;/span&gt;&#x27;;
    }
  });
}
function buildBagView(){
  _bagView=[];
  (_inv.bag||[]).forEach(function(it,idx){
    _bagView.push({kind:(it&amp;&amp;(it.kind===&#x27;resource&#x27;||it.fartSlag===true||it.monsterCore===true)?&#x27;resource&#x27;:&#x27;gear&#x27;),it:it,bagIndex:idx});

function makeSwipe(view,track){
  // V209: native scrolling only. The old transform/pointer swipe fought
  // browser scrolling on tablets and could leave lower inventory slots unreachable.
  if(!view||!track)return;
  track.style.transform=&#x27;none&#x27;;
  track.style.transition=&#x27;none&#x27;;
  view.style.overflowY=&#x27;auto&#x27;;
  view.style.overflowX=&#x27;hidden&#x27;;
  view.style.touchAction=&#x27;pan-y&#x27;;
  view.style.webkitOverflowScrolling=&#x27;touch&#x27;;
  view._reset=()=&gt;{view.scrollTop=0;};
}
makeSwipe(invViewport,invTrack);

function makeSwipe(view,track){
  // V209: native scrolling only. The old transform/pointer swipe fought
  // browser scrolling on tablets and could leave lower inventory slots unreachable.
  if(!view||!track)return;
  track.style.transform=&#x27;none&#x27;;
  track.style.transition=&#x27;none&#x27;;
  view.style.overflowY=&#x27;auto&#x27;;
  view.style.overflowX=&#x27;hidden&#x27;;
  view.style.touchAction=&#x27;pan-y&#x27;;
  view.style.webkitOverflowScrolling=&#x27;touch&#x27;;
  view._reset=()=&gt;{view.scrollTop=0;};
}
makeSwipe(invViewport,invTrack);
makeSwipe(storeViewport,storeTrack);

function arenaFillReinforcements(){
  if(P.scene!=='arena'||!waveActive||waveNum===21||waveNum===42)return;
  const level=arenaLevelForStage(waveNum);if(!level)return;
  while(arenaWaveSpawned<arenaWaveTotal&&arenaActiveCount()<ARENA_ACTIVE_CAP)spawnArenaMob(level)
}

// Arena uses the same level 1–40 mob sets and sprites as both dungeons.

// Safe-zone building collision boxes (walls + rooftops are solid — can't walk
// into or across them, matches the art: blacksmith, warehouse, auction house,
// arena entrance structure)
const PLAZA_MASK_POLY=[
[191,293],[977,293],[983,331],[983,608],[977,713],[977,923],[959,947],[887,951],[792,951],[742,943],[697,943],[657,941],[612,941],[567,941],[522,943],[472,948],[397,951],[317,951],[268,950],[259,918],[254,879],[243,838],[231,798],[221,748],[210,693],[202,638],[195,583],[191,518],[188,448],[188,378],[189,328]
];
function plazaPointInPoly(ax,ay){
  let inside=false;
  for(let i=0,j=PLAZA_MASK_POLY.length-1;i<PLAZA_MASK_POLY.length;j=i++){
    const xi=PLAZA_MASK_POLY[i][0], yi=PLAZA_MASK_POLY[i][1];
    const xj=PLAZA_MASK_POLY[j][0], yj=PLAZA_MASK_POLY[j][1];
    const hit=((yi>ay)!=(yj>ay)) && (ax < (xj-xi)*(ay-yi)/((yj-yi)||1e-9)+xi);
    if(hit)inside=!inside;
  }
  return inside;
}
function plazaCircleWalk(wx,wy,r){
  const ax=wx/SCN_SCALE, ay=wy/SCN_SCALE, ar=r/SCN_SCALE;
  const d=ar*0.82;
  return plazaPointInPoly(ax,ay) &&
    plazaPointInPoly(ax+d,ay) && plazaPointInPoly(ax-d,ay) &&

    plazaPointInPoly(ax+d*0.7,ay+d*0.7) &&
    plazaPointInPoly(ax-d*0.7,ay+d*0.7) &&
    plazaPointInPoly(ax+d*0.7,ay-d*0.7) &&
    plazaPointInPoly(ax-d*0.7,ay-d*0.7);
}

function resolveCollision(nx,ny){
  // Only the safe zone has city/plaza collision
  if(P.scene!=='safe')return {x:nx,y:ny};
  const r=P.sz*0.28; // small foot collision radius
  let x=nx,y=ny;

const PLAZA_MASK_POLY=[
[191,293],[977,293],[983,331],[983,608],[977,713],[977,923],[959,947],[887,951],[792,951],[742,943],[697,943],[657,941],[612,941],[567,941],[522,943],[472,948],[397,951],[317,951],[268,950],[259,918],[254,879],[243,838],[231,798],[221,748],[210,693],[202,638],[195,583],[191,518],[188,448],[188,378],[189,328]
];
function plazaPointInPoly(ax,ay){
  let inside=false;
  for(let i=0,j=PLAZA_MASK_POLY.length-1;i<PLAZA_MASK_POLY.length;j=i++){
    const xi=PLAZA_MASK_POLY[i][0], yi=PLAZA_MASK_POLY[i][1];
    const xj=PLAZA_MASK_POLY[j][0], yj=PLAZA_MASK_POLY[j][1];
    const hit=((yi>ay)!=(yj>ay)) && (ax < (xj-xi)*(ay-yi)/((yj-yi)||1e-9)+xi);
    if(hit)inside=!inside;
  }
  return inside;
}
function plazaCircleWalk(wx,wy,r){
  const ax=wx/SCN_SCALE, ay=wy/SCN_SCALE, ar=r/SCN_SCALE;
  const d=ar*0.82;
  return plazaPointInPoly(ax,ay) &&
    plazaPointInPoly(ax+d,ay) && plazaPointInPoly(ax-d,ay) &&
    plazaPointInPoly(ax,ay+d) && plazaPointInPoly(ax,ay-d) &&
    plazaPointInPoly(ax+d*0.7,ay+d*0.7) &&
    plazaPointInPoly(ax-d*0.7,ay+d*0.7) &&
    plazaPointInPoly(ax+d*0.7,ay-d*0.7) &&
    plazaPointInPoly(ax-d*0.7,ay-d*0.7);
}

const BUILDINGS=[
  {x:Math.round(10*SCN_SCALE),y:Math.round(8*SCN_SCALE),w:Math.round(222*SCN_SCALE),h:Math.round(255*SCN_SCALE)},   // Blacksmith shop
  {x:Math.round(268*SCN_SCALE),y:Math.round(12*SCN_SCALE),w:Math.round(238*SCN_SCALE),h:Math.round(250*SCN_SCALE)}, // Storage warehouse (incl. crates)
  {x:Math.round(528*SCN_SCALE),y:Math.round(12*SCN_SCALE),w:Math.round(228*SCN_SCALE),h:Math.round(225*SCN_SCALE)}, // Auction house
  {x:Math.round(800*SCN_SCALE),y:Math.round(12*SCN_SCALE),w:Math.round(224*SCN_SCALE),h:Math.round(205*SCN_SCALE)}, // Arena entrance structure
];

function resolveCollision(nx,ny){
  // Only the safe zone has city/plaza collision
  if(P.scene!=='safe')return {x:nx,y:ny};
  const r=P.sz*0.28; // small foot collision radius
  let x=nx,y=ny;

  // Plaza mask: keep the player's feet on the paved square.
  // Slide along the edge instead of snagging on it.
  if(!plazaCircleWalk(x,y,r)){
    if(plazaCircleWalk(nx,P.y,r)){ x=nx; y=P.y; }
    else if(plazaCircleWalk(P.x,ny,r)){ x=P.x; y=ny; }
    else { x=P.x; y=P.y; }
  }
  for(const b of BUILDINGS){
    const cx0=b.x-r,cy0=b.y-r,cx1=b.x+b.w+r,cy1=b.y+b.h+r;
    if(x>cx0&&x<cx1&&y>cy0&&y<cy1){
      // Push out along the shortest axis (simple AABB resolution)
      const left=x-cx0,right=cx1-x,top=y-cy0,bottom=cy1-y;
      const min=Math.min(left,right,top,bottom);
      if(min===left)x=cx0;
      else if(min===right)x=cx1;
      else if(min===top)y=cy0;
      else y=cy1;
    }
  }
  return {x,y};
}

function updateJoystickFromPointer(e){
  if(!jA||e.pointerId!==jPointerId)return;
  e.preventDefault();
  const r=joy.getBoundingClientRect();
  const cx=r.left+r.width/2,cy=r.top+r.height/2;
  let dx=e.clientX-cx,dy=e.clientY-cy;
  const d=Math.hypot(dx,dy),mx=50;
  if(d>mx){dx=dx/d*mx;dy=dy/d*mx}
  jX=dx/mx;jY=dy/mx;
  jS.style.left=(37+dx)+'px';jS.style.top=(37+dy)+'px';
}
function jPointerDown(e){
  // Ignore extra fingers: only the first pointer that starts on the joystick owns it.
  if(jPointerId!==null)return;
  e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();
  // V139: joystick no longer cancels Smart Attack.
  // While the player is steering manually, target lock remains active.
  jPointerId=e.pointerId;jA=true;
  try{joy.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId)}catch(_){}
  updateJoystickFromPointer(e);
}
function jPointerMove(e){
  if(e.pointerId!==jPointerId)return;
  e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();
  updateJoystickFromPointer(e);
}
function jPointerEnd(e){
  if(e.pointerId!==jPointerId)return;
  e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();
  try{if(joy.hasPointerCapture(e.pointerId))joy.releasePointerCapture(e.pointerId)}catch(_){}
  resetJoystick(e.pointerId);
}

joy.addEventListener('pointerdown',jPointerDown,{passive:false});
joy.addEventListener('pointermove',jPointerMove,{passive:false});
joy.addEventListener('pointerup',jPointerEnd,{passive:false});

}

function blackMarketOfferIcon(o){
  if(!o)return '◆';
  if(o.kind==='statRune'){var _rd=runeDefByKey(o.refId);return _rd?_rd.icon:'◇'}
  if(o.kind==='stone')return o.refId==='rune'?'ᚱ':'✦';
  if(o.kind==='feather')return '🔥';
  if(o.kind==='pet')return '🐾';
  if(o.kind==='wings')return '🪽';
  if(o.slot==='cloak')return '🧥';
  if(o.slot==='necklace')return '📿';
  if(o.slot==='artifact')return '🔮';
  if(o.kind==='grimoire')return '📘';
  if(o.kind==='consumable')return o.refId==='portal_scroll'?'📜':'✦';
  return '◆'
}

function blackMarketAfterChange(){
  saveGame();sendInvState();sendBlackMarketState();sendBlacksmithState();sendMerchantState();sendAuctionState();updateUI()
}
function blackMarketBuy(id){
  var bm=blackMarketBuildState(false),o=(bm.offers||[]).find(function(x){return x.id===id});
  if(!o){blackMarketNotice('Предложение уже недоступно');sendBlackMarketState();return}
  var bought=(bm.bought&&bm.bought[id])||0,limit=o.limit||1;
  if(bought>=limit){blackMarketNotice('Этот товар на сегодня распродан');return}
  if(blackMarketNeedsBag(o)&&(INV.bag||[]).length>=100){blackMarketNotice('Инвентарь заполнен 100 / 100');return}
  var price=Number(o.price)||0,cur=o.currency==='gram'?'gram':'ppa';
  if(cur==='gram'&&(INV.gram||0)<price){blackMarketNotice('Недостаточно Gram');return}
  if(cur==='ppa'&&(INV.ppa||0)<price){blackMarketNotice('Недостаточно PPA');return}
  if(cur==='gram'){INV.gram=Math.round(((INV.gram||0)-price)*100)/100;if(typeof recordGramSpend==='function')recordGramSpend(price)}
  else INV.ppa=(INV.ppa||0)-price;
  if(!blackMarketGrant(o,o.qty||1)){if(cur==='gram')INV.gram+=price;else INV.ppa+=price;blackMarketNotice('Не удалось выдать предмет');return}
  bm.bought=bm.bought||{};bm.bought[id]=bought+1;
  if(id==='portal_scroll_bundle_5')INV.blackMarketPortalScrollReadyAt=Date.now()+48*60*60*1000;
  blackMarketAfterChange();blackMarketNotice('Куплено: '+o.name);showPickup('Блек Маркет · '+o.name,'#d78b91')
}
function blackMarketMysteryReward(){
  var mats=blackMarketMaterialPool(),gr=blackMarketCurrentGrimoires(),r=Math.random();
  if(r<0.23)return {kind:'stone',refId:'premium',name:'Премиум камень заточки ×2',qty:2};
  if(r<0.38)return {kind:'stone',refId:'rune',name:'Премиум руна заточки',qty:1};
  if(r<0.52)return {kind:'feather',refId:'phoenix',name:'Перо Феникса',qty:1};
  if(r<0.70)return {kind:'consumable',refId:'xp_scroll',name:'Свиток опыта ×2',qty:2};
  if(r<0.88&&mats[0])return {kind:'material',refId:mats[0],name:mats[0]+' ×4',qty:4};
  if(gr.length){var g=gr[Math.floor(Math.random()*gr.length)];return {kind:'grimoire',refId:g.id,name:g.n||'Гримуар',qty:1}}
  return {kind:'stone',refId:'premium',name:'Премиум камень заточки ×3',qty:3}
}

function blackMarketSellResource(name,qty){
  var bm=blackMarketBuildState(false),x=(bm.buyback||[]).find(function(v){return v.name===name});
  if(!x){blackMarketNotice('Сегодня этот ресурс не принимают');return}
  bm.sold=bm.sold||{};var sold=bm.sold[name]||0,remaining=Math.max(0,(x.limit||99)-sold),owned=INV.materials[name]||0;
  if(remaining<=0){blackMarketNotice('Лимит скупки этого ресурса исчерпан');return}
  var n=qty==='all'?Math.min(owned,remaining):Math.min(Math.max(1,Math.floor(Number(qty)||1)),owned,remaining);
  if(n<=0){blackMarketNotice('У тебя нет этого ресурса');return}
  INV.materials[name]=owned-n;var reward=(Number(x.payout)||0)*n;INV.ppa=(INV.ppa||0)+reward;bm.sold[name]=sold+n;
  blackMarketAfterChange();blackMarketNotice('Продано: '+name+' ×'+n+' · +'+reward+' PPA');showPickup('+'+reward+' PPA · Блек Маркет','#f0c166')
}

function openArenaMenu(){
  if(P.scene!=='safe'||transitioning)return;
  var f=document.getElementById('arenaMenuFrame');if(!f)return;
  var cf=document.getElementById('charFrame');if(cf)cf.style.display='none';
  var xf=document.getElementById('blacksmithFrame');if(xf)xf.style.display='none';
  var sf=document.getElementById('storageFrame');if(sf)sf.style.display='none';
  var mf=document.getElementById('merchantFrame');if(mf)mf.style.display='none';
  var af=document.getElementById('auctionFrame');if(af)af.style.display='none';
  var bmf=document.getElementById('blackmarketFrame');if(bmf)bmf.style.display='no
===== /build/gi #29 @ 2480383 =====
sc=SCENES[P.scene];
  nx=Math.max(sc.bounds.minX,Math.min(sc.bounds.maxX,nx));
  ny=Math.max(sc.bounds.minY,Math.min(sc.bounds.maxY,ny));

  if(P.scene==='arena'){
    // Stay on the circular arena floor — no wandering into the black void
    const c=sc.center,rad=460;
    const dx=nx-c.x,dy=ny-c.y,dist=Math.hypot(dx,dy);
    if(dist>rad){nx=c.x+dx/dist*rad;ny=c.y+dy/dist*rad}
  }else if(P.scene==='pvp1'||P.scene==='pvpteam'){
    const mv=pvpSlide(P.scene,P.x,P.y,nx-P.x,ny-P.y,P.sz*0.24);nx=mv.x;ny=mv.y;
  }else if(P.scene==='dungeon'){
    // Dungeon walls — slide along corridors, never clip through
    const mv=dgSlide(P.x,P.y,nx-P.x,ny-P.y,P.sz*0.24);
    nx=mv.x;ny=mv.y;
  }else if(P.scene==='clansiege'){
    const mv=clanSiegeSlide(P.x,P.y,nx-P.x,ny-P.y,P.sz*0.24);
    nx=mv.x;ny=mv.y;
  }else if(P.scene==='clanboss1'){
    const mv=clanBossMoveWithBodyCollision(P.x,P.y,nx-P.x,ny-P.y,P.sz*0.24);
    nx=mv.x;ny=mv.y;
  }else if(P.scene==='worldboss'){
    const mv=worldBossMoveWithCrystalCollision(P.x,P.y,nx-P.x,ny-P.y,P.sz*0.24);
    nx=mv.x;ny=mv.y;
  }else if(P.scene==='fartzone'){
    // Exact user-drawn meadow boundary: outside = solid collision.
    // Do not alter town/building hitboxes or other scenes.
    if(!pointInPolygonFartZone(nx,ny)){
      nx=P.x;ny=P.y;
    }
  }else{
    // Solid buildings — can't walk through walls or across rooftops
    const resolved=resolveCollision(nx,ny);
    nx=resolved.x;ny=resolved.y;
  }
  // V104: keep the player's visible foot shadow outside Phoenix/mob shadows.
  // The map/wall collision above remains authoritative; this only adds body blocking.
  if(P.scene==='dungeon'||P.scene==='arena'){
    const _bodyMv=ppaMoveWithWorldBodyCollision(P.x,P.y,nx-P.x,ny-P.y,P.sz*.24);
    nx=_bodyMv.x;ny=_bodyMv.y;
  }
  P.x=nx;P.y=ny;
  if(P.scene==='clansiege')clanSiegeUpdateCapture();

  // Portal collision
  if(P.portalCD>0)P.portalCD--;
  if(P.portalCD<=0&&!transitioning&&sc.portal){
    const p=sc.portal;
    if(Math.hypot(P.x-p.x,P.y-p.y)<p.r+20){
      changeScene(p.target);
    }
  }
  // Town teleport -> dungeon (walk into the rune circle)
  if(P.portalCD<=0&&!transitioning&&P.scene==='safe'&&sc.teleport&&sc.teleport.target){
    const tp=sc.teleport;
    if(Math.hypot(P.x-tp.x,P.y-tp.y)<tp.r){
      changeScene(tp.target);
    }
  }
  // 1x1 duel preparation: both players may move, but PvP damage stays locked for 10 seconds.
  updatePvpDuelWarmup();

  // Arena ladder: only a fully cleared stage advances.
  if(P.scene==='arena'){
    if(waveActive&&arenaWaveKilled>=arenaWaveTotal&&arenaActiveCount()===0){
      arenaRecordWave(arenaStageProgress());
      waveActive=false;
      waveTimer=180; // 3 sec break: enough to read the HUD, short enough to keep pace
    }
    if(!waveActive&&waveTimer>0){
      waveTimer--;
      if(waveTimer<=0)startWave();
    }
  }

  if(Math.abs(P.vx)>0.1||Math.abs(P.vy)>0.1)P.bob+=0.28;
  else P.bob+=0.04;

  if(P.shootT<=0){
    // Update facing based on horizontal movement for side sprite flip
    if(P.vx > 0.1) P.face = 1;
    else if(P.vx < -0.1) P.face = -1;
    // When moving vertically, keep last horizontal facing
  }

  if(P.shootCD>0)P.shootCD--;
  if(P.shootT>0)P.shootT-=0.12;
  if(P.recoil>0)P.recoil-=0.1;

  if(attackQueued&&P.shootCD<=0){
    attackQueued=false;

    // Preserve the joystick state. Combat must never cancel movement.
    const movingNow=playerIsMoving();

    if(playerUsesGnomeSprites()){

function ppaCanRebuildItemArt(it){
  if(!it||!it.slot)return false;
  if(it.slot==='pet'){
    var pn=it.petName||it.name;
    return !!(pn&&typeof PET_DIR_ART!=='undefined'&&PET_DIR_ART[pn]);
  }
  if(it.slot==='wings'){
    return !!(typeof WING_DIR_ART!=='undefined'&&(WING_DIR_ART[it.rarity]||WING_DIR_ART.rare));
  }
  if(it.slot==='cloak'||it.slot==='artifact')return true;
  if(it.slot==='necklace')return true;
  return !!(typeof CLASS_ART_SLOTS!=='undefined'&&CLASS_ART_SLOTS[it.slot]&&it.classKey&&it.classKey!=='all');
}

    delete o.iconArt;

  return o;
}

function ppaQueueFreshStandaloneCharacter(nickname,className){
  ppaClearLocalCharacterCacheForAccountSwitch();
  // Android content:// may recreate sessionStorage on a document reload.
  // Keep this ONE-SHOT handoff in localStorage instead, then consume/remove it
  // immediately after the clean reload. This prevents the first-start loop.
  try{
    localStorage.setItem('ppaFreshStandaloneCharacterV257',JSON.stringify({
      nickname:String(nickname||''),
      className:String(className||'')
    }));
    return true;
  }catch(_){return false}
}
function ppaConsumeFreshStandaloneCharacter(){
  try{
    var raw=localStorage.getItem('ppaFreshStandaloneCharacterV257');
    if(!raw)return null;
    localStorage.removeItem('ppaFreshStandaloneCharacterV257');
    var x=JSON.parse(raw);
    if(!x||!x.className||!/^[A-Za-zА-Яа-яЁё0-9_]{3,18}$/u.test(String(x.nickname||'').trim()))return null;
    return {nickname:String(x.nickname).trim(),className:String(x.className)};
  }catch(_){
    try{localStorage.removeItem('ppaFreshStandaloneCharacterV257')}catch(__){}
    return null;
  }
}
var PPA_V239_LAST_GOOD_WRITE=0;
function saveGame(){
  try{
    var next=ppaBuildSaveObject(),rich=ppaSaveHasCharacterState(next),nextRaw=JSON.stringify(next);
    if(!rich){
      var prevRaw=localStorage.getItem('pxSave'),prev=null;
      try{prev=prevRaw?JSON.parse(prevRaw):null}catch(_){}
      if(prev&&ppaSaveHasCharacterState(prev)){
        localStorage.setItem('pxSaveLastGood',prevRaw);
        return false;
      }
    }
    localStorage.setItem('pxSave',nextRaw);
    var now=Date.now();
    if(rich&&(now-PPA_V239_LAST_GOOD_WRITE>15000||!localStorage.getItem('pxSaveLastGood'))){
      localStorage.setItem('pxSaveLastGood',nextRaw);
      PPA_V239_LAST_GOOD_WRITE=now;
    }
    try{ppaCloudScheduleSave(next)}catch(_){}
    return true;
  }catch(e){return false}
}

const PPA_SAVE_EXPORT_KEYS=['pxSave','pxSaveLastGood','pxArenaBestWave','pxArenaShopDailyV52','pxBossAt','pxPvpDailyV51','pxPvpRatingV52'];
function ppaExportSave(){
  try{
    var data={format:'PPA_PORTABLE_SAVE_V1',createdAt:new Date().toISOString(),values:{}};
    PPA_SAVE_EXPORT_KEYS.forEach(function(k){
      var v=localStorage.getItem(k);
      if(v!==null)data.values[k]=v;
    });
    var blob=new Blob([JSON.stringify(data,null,2)],{type:'application/json'});
    var a=document.createElement('a');
    var u=URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    a.href=u;
    a.download='PPA_SAVE_BACKUP_'+new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10)+'.json';
    document.body.appendChild(a);a.click();a.remove();
    setTimeout(function(){try{URL.revokeObjectURL(u)}catch(_){}},1000);
    showPickup('СЕЙВ ЭКСПОРТИРОВАН','#7dff9f');
    return true;
  }catch(e){showPickup('НЕ УДАЛОСЬ ЭКСПОРТИРОВАТЬ СЕЙВ','#ff7777');return false}
}

function ppaCloudExtract(load){
  var root=load&&typeof load==='object'?load:{};
  var version=root.version;
  if(version==null&&root.save&&typeof root.save==='object')version=root.save.version;
  if(version==null&&root.data&&typeof root.data==='object')version=root.data.version;
  var state=root.state;
  if(state==null&&root.save&&typeof root.save==='object')state=root.save.state!=null?root.save.state:root.save.save;
  if(state==null&&root.data&&typeof root.data==='object')state=root.data.state!=null?root.data.state:root.data.save;
  if(state==null&&root.gameSave&&typeof root.gameSave==='object')state=root.gameSave.state||root.gameSave.save;
  if(typeof state==='string'){try{state=JSON.parse(state)}catch(_){state=null}}
  if(!state&&root&&typeof root==='object'&&(Array.isArray(root.bag)||root.equipped))state=root;
  return {version:version==null?null:Number(version),state:state&&typeof state==='object'?state:null};
}
function ppaCloudScheduleSave(state){
  if(!PPA_CLOUD.ready||!ppaCloudBridgeAvailable())return;
  clearTimeout(PPA_CLOUD.timer);
  var snapshot=state&&typeof state==='object'?state:ppaBuildSaveObject();
  PPA_CLOUD.timer=setTimeout(function(){ppaCloudPush(snapshot)},2500);
}
async function ppaCloudPush(state){
  if(!PPA_CLOUD.ready||!ppaCloudBridgeAvailable()||typeof PPA.ppaSaveGame!=='function')return false;
  try{
    var res=await PPA.ppaSaveGame(state||ppaBuildSaveObject(),PPA_CLOUD.version);
    if(res&&Number.isFinite(Number(res.version)))PPA_CLOUD.version=Number(res.version);
    else if(res&&res.save&&Number.isFinite(Number(res.save.version)))PPA_CLOUD.version=Number(res.save.version);
    return true;
  }catch(err){
    var now=Date.now();
    if(now-PPA_CLOUD.lastErrorAt>10000){
      PPA_CLOUD.lastErrorAt=now;
      console.warn('PPA cloud save:',err);
    }
    return false;
  }
}

function ppaMigrationSaveObject(){
  var out=ppaBuildSaveObject();
  var old=(P&&P._saved&&typeof P._saved==='object')?P._saved:null;
  if(old){
    ['lvl','xp','xn','kil','rebirths','statPts','talentPts','cls'].forEach(function(k){
      if(old[k]!=null)out[k]=old[k];
    });
    if(old.statAlloc&&typeof old.statAlloc==='object')out.statAlloc=Object.assign({},old.statAlloc);
    if(old.playerName&&!out.playerName)out.playerName=old.playerName;
  }
  return out;
}

window.PPA_UI_AUCTION_IMG=function(kind,uid,slot,rarity,classKey,className,petName,refId,name){
  try{
    kind=String(kind||'');uid=String(uid||'');slot=String(slot||'');
    rarity=String(rarity||'common');classKey=String(classKey||'');
    className=String(className||'');petName=String(petName||'');
    refId=String(refId||'');name=String(name||'');

    // For equipment, never trust legacy imported artwork stored inside the save.
    // Rebuild the image from the current approved art tables.
    if(kind==='gear'||slot){
      var own=findOwned(uid);
      if(own){
        // refreshGearArt intentionally overwrites old generated gear art.
        try{refreshGearArt(own)}catch(_){}
        slot=String(own.slot||slot||'');
        rarity=String(own.rarity||rarity||'common');
        classKey=String(own.classKey||classKey||'');
        className=String(own.className||className||'');
        petName=String(own.petName||petName||'');
        name=String(own.name||name||'');
      }

      if(slot==='ring'){
        if(rarity==='epic'&&EPIC_RING_ART&&EPIC_RING_ART.length){
          return EPIC_RING_ART[(own&&typeof stableItemArtIndex==='function')?stableItemArtIndex(own,EPIC_RING_ART.length):0]||'';
        }
        var rv=JEWELRY_VARIANTS.ring||[];
        if(rv.length){
          var ri=(own&&typeof stableItemArtIndex==='function')?stableItemArtIndex(own,rv.length):0;
          return (rv[ri]&&rv[ri].img)||'';
        }
      }
      if(slot==='necklace'){
        if(rarity==='epic'&&EPIC_NECKLACE_ART&&EPIC_NECKLACE_ART.length){
          return EPIC_NECKLACE_ART[(own&&typeof stableItemArtIndex==='function')?stableItemArtIndex(own,EPIC_NECKLACE_ART.length):0]||'';
        }
        var nv=JEWELRY_VARIANTS.necklace||[];
        if(nv.length){
          var ni=(own&&typeof stableItemArtIndex==='function')?stableItemArtIndex(own,nv.length):0;
          return (nv[ni]&&nv[ni].img)||'';
        }
      }
      if(slot==='cloak')return accessoryIconForRarity('cloak',rarity)||'';
      if(slot==='artifact')return accessoryIconForRarity('artifact',rarity)||'';
      if(slot==='wings'){
        var ws=(WING_DIR_ART&&WING_DIR_ART[rarity])||(WING_DIR_ART&&WING_DIR_ART.rare);
        return ws&&(ws.S||ws.SE||ws.E)||'';
      }
      if(slot==='pet'||kind==='pet'){
        var ps=(PET_DIR_ART&&PET_DIR_ART[petName||name])||null;
        return ps&&(ps.S||ps.SE||ps.E)||'';
      }

      if(!classKey&&className&&typeof classKeyFromName==='function')classKey=classKeyFromName(className)||'';

      // imported player-owned item: current class is the safest visual fallback.

function kqRender(){
  const p=kqEnsureUi();if(!p)return;kqState();
  if(!KQ_ACTIVE_TAB){const lv=Number(P.lvl)||1;KQ_ACTIVE_TAB=lv>=31?'31-40':lv>=21?'21-30':'1-20'}
  p.querySelectorAll('[data-kqtab]').forEach(function(b){b.classList.toggle('on',b.dataset.kqtab===KQ_ACTIVE_TAB);const g=KQ_GROUPS[b.dataset.kqtab];b.classList.toggle('locked',!!g&&Number(P.lvl)<g.min)});
  const body=document.getElementById('keeperQuestBody');if(!body)return;
  if(KQ_ACTIVE_TAB==='daily'){body.innerHTML=kqDailyBody();return}
  const g=KQ_GROUPS[KQ_ACTIVE_TAB]||KQ_GROUPS['1-20'];
  body.innerHTML=g.ids.map(kqQuestCard).join('')+kqChainCard(KQ_ACTIVE_TAB)+'<div class="keeperQuestNote">Разовые поручения и их награды можно получить только один раз на персонажа. Прогресс сохраняется.</div>';
}

<!-- V130 PAID CLASS CHANGE
- Premium Shop -> Premium-status section now contains "Смена класса" for 10 Gram.
- Allowed only in Мирном городе.
- Level, XP, rebirths, currencies, inventory and account progress are kept.
- Earned stat points are refunded for redistribution on the new class.
- Equipped + bag + personal/Premium-storage gear of the OLD class is converted
  to equivalent NEW-class gear while preserving rarity, enhancement, rolled
  stats, item level, BM and UID.
- Clan storage and auction lots are never converted.
- Converted gear becomes account-bound and is excluded from auction/direct trade
  to prevent market arbitrage through repeated class changes.
- This purchase spends Gram but does NOT count as a verified lifetime paid top-up.
- The old temporary startup reset is removed; paid class change uses persistent progression.
-->

<!-- V131 FULL CLASS BALANCE L1-L40
- One authoritative CLASS_BALANCE table for all 8 classes.
- Every class has clean level-1 and clean level-40:
  HP, MP, ATK, DEF, movement speed, attack speed, basic range,
  crit chance, dodge and crit damage.
- Stats interpolate every level from 1 to 40; lvl40+ uses lvl40 clean baseline.
- Gear and allocated stat points are applied ON TOP of clean class stats.
- Normal attack no longer has the old hidden +10 damage.
- V135 supersedes the old fixed-hit-count idea. Player classes are role-balanced,
  not tuned to kill a particular mob in a particular number of hits.
- Gnome cannonball now uses NORMAL attack damage, not skill damage.
- Basic target acquisition obeys the current class's attack range.
- Attack cooldown obeys the current class's attack speed.
- Movement now actually follows the recomputed class movement speed.
- Old universal +20 HP / +10 MP per level removed.
- Paid class change and fresh sprite/class test use the same central balance table.
-->

// V34: existing Black Market NPC opens its own working menu shell; town coordinates and map unchanged.
// V33: full client purchase dialog + async server bridge; no fake transactions are created locally.
// V32: official minimum auction prices; seller can raise price, game rejects/lifts anything below the item floor.
// V30: completed client-side auction shell; map/city assets intentionally untouched.
function auctionRarityName(r){return ({common:'Обычный',uncommon:'Необычный',rare:'Редкий',epic:'Эпический',legendary:'Легендарный'})[r]||'Обычный'}
function auctionMinPrices(it){
  it=it||{};
  var gear=it.gear||{},kind=it.kind||'',id=it.refId||'';
  var slot=it.slot||gear.slot||'';
  var rarity=it.rarity||gear.rarity||'common';
  var level=Number(it.level||it.lvl||gear.level||gear.lvl||0)||0;
  var gram=0.10,ppa=1;

  // Pets: user-defined market floor. Craft PPA cost is also the PPA floor.
  if(slot==='pet'){
    gram=({common:2,uncommon:4,rare:10})[rarity]||2;
    ppa=({common:500,uncommon:1200,rare:3000})[rarity]||500;
  }
  // Special craft accessories: floor follows their blacksmith PPA price.
  // Gram uses the simple auction conversion 1000 PPA = 1 Gram.
  else if(slot==='cloak'||slot==='necklace'||slot==='artifact'||slot==='wings'){
    var specialPpa={
      cloak:{common:200,uncommon:600,rare:1800,epic:5000},
      necklace:{common:800,uncommon:1800,rare:4000,epic:8000},
      artifact:{common:1000,uncommon:2200,rare:5000,epic:9000},
      wings:{common:1200,uncommon:2800,rare:6000,epic:10000}
    };
    ppa=(specialPpa[slot]&&specialPpa[slot][rarity])||1;
    gram=Math.max(0.10,ppa/1000);
  }
  // Normal equipment.
  else if(kind==='gear'||slot){
    gram=({common:0.10,uncommon:0.50,rare:3.00,epic:7.00,legendary:15.00})[rarity]||0.10;
    ppa=({common:1,uncommon:30,rare:100,epic:12000,legendary:20000})[rarity]||1;
    // Crafted epic weapon/armor cost 15k PPA; other epic gear costs 12k.
    if(rarity==='epic'&&(slot==='weapon'||slot==='armor'))ppa=15000;
  }
  // Consumables/resources keep the current V31 floor, but can now be priced higher.
  else if(kind==='consumable'){
    var cg={hp_small:0.30,hp_medium:0.35,hp_large:0.45,mp_small:0.30,mp_medium:0.35,mp_large:0.45,magic_small:0.50,atk_speed_small:0.50,run_speed_small:0.50,phys_small:0.50,xp_scroll:0.60};
    var cp={hp_small:1,hp_medium:1,hp_large:1,mp_small:1,mp_medium:1,mp_large:1,magic_small:1,atk_speed_small:1,run_speed_small:1,phys_small:1,xp_scroll:2};
    gram=cg[id]||0.30;ppa=cp[id]||1;
  }else if(kind==='stone'){
    gram=({normal:0.35,premium:0.70,rune:1.20})[id]||0.35;
    ppa=({normal:1,premium:2,rune:3})[id]||1;
  }else if(kind==='feather'){gram=1.50;ppa=4}
  else if(kind==='grimoire'){gram=1.20;ppa=3}
  else if(kind==='material'){
    gram=({common:0.30,uncommon:0.40,rare:0.60,epic:0.90,legendary:1.30})[rarity]||0.30;
    ppa=({common:1,uncommon:1,rare:2,epic:3,legendary:4})[rarity]||1;
  }

  gram=Math.round(Math.max(0.10,gram)*100)/100;
  ppa=Math.max(1,Math.round(ppa));
  return {gram:gram,ppa:ppa};
}
function auctionAttachMinPrices(it){var p=auctionMinPrices(it);it.minGram=p.gram;it.minPpa=p.ppa;return it}

function auctionCategoryForGear(it){
  var s=it&&it.slot||'';
  if(s==='weapon')return 'weapon';
  if(['helmet','armor','gloves','legs','boots'].indexOf(s)>=0)return 'armor';
  if(['ring','necklace','artifact','cloak','wings','pet'].indexOf(s)>=0)return 'accessories';
  return 'misc';
}

===== /version/gi #2 @ 2732949 =====
LoopStarted=false;
function startGame(){
  if(gameStarted)return;
  gameStarted=true;
  try{loadBarFill.style.width='100%';if(loadPct)loadPct.textContent='100%';loadText.textContent='Готово';loadDetail.textContent='Мир готов';}catch(_){}
  loadingScreen.style.transition='opacity 0.5s';
  loadingScreen.style.opacity='0';
  setTimeout(function(){
    loadingScreen.style.display='none';
    Promise.resolve(typeof ppaReleaseGatewayBootstrap==='function'?ppaReleaseGatewayBootstrap():false)
      .then(function(handled){
        if(handled)return;
        var fresh=ppaConsumeFreshStandaloneCharacter();
        if(fresh){
          INV.playerName=fresh.nickname;window.PPA_PLAYER_NAME=fresh.nickname;
          try{localStorage.setItem('ppaPlayerNameV205',fresh.nickname)}catch(_){}
          applyClass({name:fresh.className});beginGame();
          return;
        }
        document.getElementById('classSelectFrame').style.display='block';
      })
      .catch(function(){
        document.getElementById('classSelectFrame').style.display='block';
      });
  },500);
}

// === V219 GATEWAY / CLOUD SAVE BRIDGE ====================================
const PPA_CLOUD={
  ready:false,loading:false,version:null,timer:0,lastErrorAt:0,profile:null
};
function ppaCloudBridgeAvailable(){
  try{return !!(window.PPA&&typeof PPA.isAvailable==='function'&&PPA.isAvailable())}catch(_){return false}
}
function ppaCloudExtract(load){
  var root=load&&typeof load==='object'?load:{};
  var version=root.version;
  if(version==null&&root.save&&typeof root.save==='object')version=root.save.version;
  if(version==null&&root.data&&typeof root.data==='object')version=root.data.version;
  var state=root.state;
  if(state==null&&root.save&&typeof root.save==='object')state=root.save.state!=null?root.save.state:root.save.save;
  if(state==null&&root.data&&typeof root.data==='object')state=root.data.state!=null?root.data.state:root.data.save;
  if(state==null&&root.gameSave&&typeof root.gameSave==='object')state=root.gameSave.state||root.gameSave.save;
  if(typeof state==='string'){try{state=JSON.parse(state)}catch(_){state=null}}
  if(!state&&root&&typeof root==='object'&&(Array.isArray(root.bag)||root.equipped))state=root;
  return {version:version==null?null:Number(version),state:state&&typeof state==='object'?state:null};
}
function ppaCloudScheduleSave(state){
  if(!PPA_CLOUD.ready||!ppaCloudBridgeAvailable())return;
  clearTimeout(PPA_CLOUD.timer);
  var snapshot=state&&typeof state==='object'?state:ppaBuildSaveObject();
  PPA_CLOUD.timer=setTimeout(function(){ppaCloudPush(snapshot)},2500);
}
async function ppaCloudPush(state){
  if(!PPA_CLOUD.ready||!ppaCloudBridgeAvailable()||typeof PPA.ppaSaveGame!=='function')return false;
  try{
    var res=await PPA.ppaSaveGame(state||ppaBuildSaveObject(),PPA_CLOUD.version);
    if(res&&Number.isFinite(Number(res.version)))PPA_CLOUD.version=Number(res.version);
    else if(res&&res.save&&Number.isFinite(Number(res.save.version)))PPA_CLOUD.version=Number(res.save.version);
    return true;
  }catch(err){
    var now=Date.now();
    if(now-PPA_CLOUD.lastErrorAt>10000){
      PPA_CLOUD.lastErrorAt=now;
      console.warn('PPA cloud save:',err);
    }
    return false;
  }
}
function ppaProfileClassKey(profile){
  profile=profile&&typeof profile==='object'?profile:{};
  var raw=profile.classKey||profile.class_key||profile.characterClass||profile.character_class||profile.cls||profile.className||profile.class_name||'';
  raw=String(raw||'').trim();
  var aliases={
    'guardian':'tank','guard':'tank','страж':'tank',
    'paladin':'palad

const PPA_CLOUD={
  ready:false,loading:false,version:null,timer:0,lastErrorAt:0,profile:null
};
function ppaCloudBridgeAvailable(){
  try{return !!(window.PPA&&typeof PPA.isAvailable==='function'&&PPA.isAvailable())}catch(_){return false}
}
function ppaCloudExtract(load){
  var root=load&&typeof load==='object'?load:{};
  var version=root.version;
  if(version==null&&root.save&&typeof root.save==='object')version=root.save.version;
  if(version==null&&root.data&&typeof root.data==='object')version=root.data.version;
  var state=root.state;
  if(state==null&&root.save&&typeof root.save==='object')state=root.save.state!=null?root.save.state:root.save.save;
  if(state==null&&root.data&&typeof root.data==='object')state=root.data.state!=null?root.data.state:root.data.save;
  if(state==null&&root.gameSave&&typeof root.gameSave==='object')state=root.gameSave.state||root.gameSave.save;
  if(typeof state==='string'){try{state=JSON.parse(state)}catch(_){state=null}}
  if(!state&&root&&typeof root==='object'&&(Array.isArray(root.bag)||root.equipped))state=root;
  return {version:version==null?null:Number(version),state:state&&typeof state==='object'?state:null};
}
function ppaCloudScheduleSave(state){
  if(!PPA_CLOUD.ready||!ppaCloudBridgeAvailable())return;
  clearTimeout(PPA_CLOUD.timer);
  var snapshot=state&&typeof state==='object'?state:ppaBuildSaveObject();
  PPA_CLOUD.timer=setTimeout(function(){ppaCloudPush(snapshot)},2500);
}
async function ppaCloudPush(state){
  if(!PPA_CLOUD.ready||!ppaCloudBridgeAvailable()||typeof PPA.ppaSaveGame!=='function')return false;
  try{
    var res=await PPA.ppaSaveGame(state||ppaBuildSaveObject(),PPA_CLOUD.version);
    if(res&&Number.isFinite(Number(res.version)))PPA_CLOUD.version=Number(res.version);
    else if(res&&res.save&&Number.isFinite(Number(res.save.version)))PPA_CLOUD.version=Number(res.save.version);
    return true;
  }catch(err){
    var now=Date.now();
    if(now-PPA_CLOUD.lastErrorAt>10000){
      PPA_CLOUD.lastErrorAt=now;
      console.warn('PPA cloud save:',err);
    }
    return false;
  }
}

async function ppaCloudPush(state){
  if(!PPA_CLOUD.ready||!ppaCloudBridgeAvailable()||typeof PPA.ppaSaveGame!=='function')return false;
  try{
    var res=await PPA.ppaSaveGame(state||ppaBuildSaveObject(),PPA_CLOUD.version);
    if(res&&Number.isFinite(Number(res.version)))PPA_CLOUD.version=Number(res.version);
    else if(res&&res.save&&Number.isFinite(Number(res.save.version)))PPA_CLOUD.version=Number(res.save.version);
    return true;
  }catch(err){
    var now=Date.now();
    if(now-PPA_CLOUD.lastErrorAt>10000){
      PPA_CLOUD.lastErrorAt=now;
      console.warn('PPA cloud save:',err);
    }
    return false;
  }
}

async function ppaReleaseGatewayBootstrap(){
  if(!ppaCloudBridgeAvailable())return false;
  if(PPA_CLOUD.loading)return true;
  PPA_CLOUD.loading=true;
  try{
    if(typeof PPA.ppaAuthTelegram==='function')await PPA.ppaAuthTelegram();
    var profile=typeof PPA.ppaLoadProfile==='function'?await PPA.ppaLoadProfile():null;
    profile=(profile&&profile.profile)||profile||{};
    PPA_CLOUD.profile=profile;

    var load=typeof PPA.ppaLoadSave==='function'?await PPA.ppaLoadSave():null;
    var cloud=ppaCloudExtract(load);
    PPA_CLOUD.version=cloud.version;

    var forceMigration=localStorage.getItem('ppaCloudForceMigrationV219')==='1';
    var profileNick=ppaProfileNickname(profile);
    var savedNick=ppaLocalSavedNickname();
    var localCachePresent=ppaHasAnyLocalCharacterCache();
    var cloudHasCharacter=ppaSaveHasCharacterState(cloud.state);

    // Explicit portable-save import always wins: the player deliberately chose it.
    if(forceMigration&&typeof PPA.ppaSaveGame==='function'){
      var migrationState=null;
      if(false){
        var migrated=await PPA.ppaSaveGame(migrationState,cloud.version);
        if(migrated&&Number.isFinite(Number(migrated.version)))PPA_CLOUD.version=Number(migrated.version);
        else if(migrated&&migrated.save&&Number.isFinite(Number(migrated.save.version)))PPA_CLOUD.version=Number(migrated.save.version);
        localStorage.removeItem('ppaCloudForceMigrationV219');
        cloud={version:PPA_CLOUD.version,state:migrationState};
        cloudHasCharacter=true;
      }
    }

    // Existing cloud character is authoritative for this Telegram account.
    // Apply it directly to memory: no second resource-loading screen.
    if(cloudHasCharacter&&!forceMigration){
      var stamp='v'+String(cloud.version==null?'na':cloud.version);
      if(sessionStorage.getItem('ppaCloudLoadedStamp')!==stamp){
        localStorage.setItem('pxSave',JSON.stringify(cloud.state));
        localStorage.setItem('pxSaveLastGood',JSON.stringify(cloud.state));
        if(cloud.state.playerName)try{localStorage.setItem('ppaPlayerNameV205',String(cloud.state.playerName))}catch(_){}
        sessionStorage.setItem('ppaCloudLoadedStamp',stamp);
        if(!loadGame())console.warn('PPA: cloud save could not be applied in memory');
      }
    }

    // Empty cloud + old local cache is the dangerous case. Migrate only when
    // the local nickname belongs to the same profile. Otherwise this is a new
    // Telegram account on a device that still contains another player's cache.
    if(!cloudHasCharacter&&localCachePresent&&!forceMigration){
      var savedNickOk0=/^[A-Za-zА-Яа-яЁё0-9_]{3,18}$/u.test(savedNick);
      var profileNickOk0=/^[A-Za-zА-Яа-яЁё0-9_]{3,18}$/u.test(profileNick);
      if(savedNickOk0&&profileNickOk0&&ppaNickKey(savedNick)===ppaNickKey(profileNick)&&typeof PPA.ppaSaveGame==='function'){
        var initialState=ppaMigrationSaveObject();
        var fir
===== /version/gi #22 @ 2737788 =====
ding:18px;background:#160d0c">PHOENIX PIX ARENA<br><br><span style="color:#c9b9a1;font:11px monospace">'+String(msg||'Ошибка Telegram Gateway')+'</span></div>';
  }catch(_){}
}

  var old=(P&&P._saved&&typeof P._saved==='object')?P._saved:null;

    ['lvl','xp','xn','kil','rebirths','statPts','talentPts','cls'].forEach(function(k){
      if(old[k]!=null)out[k]=old[k];

async function ppaReleaseGatewayBootstrap(){
  if(!ppaCloudBridgeAvailable())return false;
  if(PPA_CLOUD.loading)return true;
  PPA_CLOUD.loading=true;
  try{
    if(typeof PPA.ppaAuthTelegram==='function')await PPA.ppaAuthTelegram();
    var profile=typeof PPA.ppaLoadProfile==='function'?await PPA.ppaLoadProfile():null;
    profile=(profile&&profile.profile)||profile||{};
    PPA_CLOUD.profile=profile;

    var load=typeof PPA.ppaLoadSave==='function'?await PPA.ppaLoadSave():null;
    var cloud=ppaCloudExtract(load);
    PPA_CLOUD.version=cloud.version;

    var forceMigration=localStorage.getItem('ppaCloudForceMigrationV219')==='1';
    var profileNick=ppaProfileNickname(profile);
    var savedNick=ppaLocalSavedNickname();
    var localCachePresent=ppaHasAnyLocalCharacterCache();
    var cloudHasCharacter=ppaSaveHasCharacterState(cloud.state);

    // Explicit portable-save import always wins: the player deliberately chose it.
    if(forceMigration&&typeof PPA.ppaSaveGame==='function'){
      var migrationState=null;
      if(false){
        var migrated=await PPA.ppaSaveGame(migrationState,cloud.version);
        if(migrated&&Number.isFinite(Number(migrated.version)))PPA_CLOUD.version=Number(migrated.version);
        else if(migrated&&migrated.save&&Number.isFinite(Number(migrated.save.version)))PPA_CLOUD.version=Number(migrated.save.version);
        localStorage.removeItem('ppaCloudForceMigrationV219');
        cloud={version:PPA_CLOUD.version,state:migrationState};
        cloudHasCharacter=true;
      }
    }

    // Existing cloud character is authoritative for this Telegram account.
    // Apply it directly to memory: no second resource-loading screen.
    if(cloudHasCharacter&&!forceMigration){
      var stamp='v'+String(cloud.version==null?'na':cloud.version);
      if(sessionStorage.getItem('ppaCloudLoadedStamp')!==stamp){
        localStorage.setItem('pxSave',JSON.stringify(cloud.state));
        localStorage.setItem('pxSaveLastGood',JSON.stringify(cloud.state));
        if(cloud.state.playerName)try{localStorage.setItem('ppaPlayerNameV205',String(cloud.state.playerName))}catch(_){}
        sessionStorage.setItem('ppaCloudLoadedStamp',stamp);
        if(!loadGame())console.warn('PPA: cloud save could not be applied in memory');
      }
    }

    // Empty cloud + old local cache is the dangerous case. Migrate only when
    // the local nickname belongs to the same profile. Otherwise this is a new
    // Telegram account on a device that still contains another player's cache.
    if(!cloudHasCharacter&&localCachePresent&&!forceMigration){
      var savedNickOk0=/^[A-Za-zА-Яа-яЁё0-9_]{3,18}$/u.test(savedNick);
      var profileNickOk0=/^[A-Za-zА-Яа-яЁё0-9_]{3,18}$/u.test(profileNick);
      if(savedNickOk0&&profileNickOk0&&ppaNickKey(savedNick)===ppaNickKey(profileNick)&&typeof PPA.ppaSaveGame==='function'){
        var initialState=ppaMigrationSaveObject();
        var first=await PPA.ppaSaveGame(initialState,cloud.version);
        if(first&&Number.isFinite(Number(first.version)))PPA_CLOUD.version=Number(first.version);
        else if(first&&first.save&&Number.isFinite(Number(first.save.version)))PPA_CLOUD.version=Number(first.save.version);
        cloud={version:PPA_CLOUD.version,state:initialState};
        cloudHasCharacter=true;
      }else{
        console.info('PPA: new Telegram account detected; clearing foreign local character cache without reload.');
        ppaClearLocalCharacterCacheForAccountSwitch();
        ppaResetFreshCharacterInMemory(profileNick);
        savedNick=profileNick;
  
===== /version/gi #25 @ 2738435 =====

      return oldApplyEnhancementStats?oldApplyEnhancementStats(it):it;

      next[k]=sharpenRoundStat(k,n+v214EnhValue(it,k,lvl));

    it.stats=next;
    applyAwakeningExtras(it,lvl);
    // Standard gear is not a pet, but leave the hook harmless and future-safe.
    if(typeof applyPetEnhancementExtras==='function')applyPetEnhancementExtras(it,lvl);

'use strict';

  const V278_LEGENDARY_BASE={
    weapon:{atk:525,crit:39},
    helmet:{def:174,hp:540,magicResist:18},
    armor:{def:270,hp:960,magicResist:18},
    gloves:{def:132,atkSpeedPct:30,magicResist:18},
    ring:{atk:165,crit:36},
    legs:{def:162,spd:15,magicResist:18},
    boots:{def:114,spd:21,magicResist:18}
  };
  const V278_STANDARD={weapon:1,helmet:1,armor:1,gloves:1,ring:1,legs:1,boots:1};

  function clone(o){
    const n={};o=o||{};
    for(const k in o){
      const v=Number(o[k]);
      if(Number.isFinite(v))n[k]=v;
    }
    return n;
  }

  // Final craftStats wrapper: do NOT let the old V214 standard-gear wrapper
  // swallow legendary and return an empty stat object.
  const previousCraftStats=window.craftStats;
  window.craftStats=function(kind,slot,name,rarity){
    if(kind==='gear'&&rarity==='legendary'&&V278_STANDARD[slot]){
      return clone(V278_LEGENDARY_BASE[slot]);
    }
    return previousCraftStats?previousCraftStats.apply(this,arguments):{};
  };
  try{craftStats=window.craftStats}catch(_){}

  function normalizeOne(it,force){
    if(!it||it.rarity!=='legendary'||!V278_STANDARD[it.slot])return false;
    if(!force&&Number(it.legendaryBalanceVersion)===278)return false;

    const base=clone(V278_LEGENDARY_BASE[it.slot]);
    if(!Object.keys(base).length)return false;

    it.enhBaseStats=clone(base);
    it.stats=clone(base);
    it.legendaryBalanceVersion=278;

    // Preserve the item's current +0..+7 enhancement, applying it on top
    // of the corrected legendary base.
    try{
      if(typeof applyEnhancementStats==='function')applyEnhancementStats(it);
      else if(typeof syncItemBM==='function')syncItemBM(it);
    }catch(_){
      try{if(typeof syncItemBM==='function')syncItemBM(it)}catch(__){}
    }
    return true;
  }

  function eachOwned(fn){
    (INV.bag||[]).forEach(fn);
    if(INV.equipped){
      for(const k in INV.equipped)fn(INV.equipped[k]);
    }
    if(INV.storage){
      ['personal','clan','premium'].forEach(k=>(INV.storage[k]||[]).forEach(fn));
    }
    (INV.auctionLots||[]).forEach(l=>{
      if(!l)return;
      fn(l.item&&l.item.gear?l.item.gear:l.item);
    });
  }

  function migrateAll(){
    let changed=0;
    eachOwned(it=>{if(normalizeOne(it,false))changed++});
    if(changed){
      try{recomputeStats()}catch(_){}
      try{syncAllItemBM()}catch(_){}
      try{saveGame()}catch(_){}
      try{sendInvState()}catch(_){}
      try{sendBlacksmithState()}catch(_){}
      try{sendStorageState()}catch(_){}
    }
    return changed;
  }
  window.v278NormalizeLegendaryGear=normalizeOne;
  window.v278MigrateLegendaryGear=migrateAll;

  const oldLoad=window.loadGame;
  if(typeof oldLoad==='function'){
    window.loadGame=function(){
      const r=oldLoad.apply(this,arguments);
      try{migrateAll()}catch(_){}
      return r;
    };
    try{loadGame=window.loadGame}catch(_){}
  }

  setTimeout(function(){try{migrateAll()}catch(_){}},180);
})();

    }
    (INV.auctionLots||[]).forEach(l=>{
      if(!l)return;
      fn(l.item&&l.item.gear?l.item.gear:l.item);
    });
  }

  function migrateAll(){
    let changed=0;
    eachOwned(it=>{if(normalizeOne(it,false))changed++});
    if(changed){
      try{recomputeStats()}catch(_){}
      try{syncAllItemBM()}catch(_){}
      try{saveGame()}catch(_){}
      try{sendInvState()}catch(_){}
      try{sendBlacksmithState()}catch(_){}
      try{sendStorageState()}catch(_){}
    }
    return changed;
  }
  window.v278NormalizeLegendaryGear=normalizeOne;
  window.v278MigrateLegendaryGear=migrateAll;

  const oldLoad=window.loadGame;
  if(typeof oldLoad==='function'){
    window.loadGame=function(){
      const r=oldLoad.apply(this,arguments);
      try{migrateAll()}catch(_){}
      return r;
    };
    try{loadGame=window.loadGame}catch(_){}
  }

  setTimeout(function(){try{migrateAll()}catch(_){}},180);
})();

function grimoirePreviewForSkill(sk){
  if(!sk)return ['Нет описания','Нет описания','Нет описания'];
  var exact=GRIMOIRE_EFFECT_PREVIEW[sk.id];
  if(exact)return exact.slice(0,3);

  var base=String(sk.d||'Эффект навыка будет подключён вместе с механикой класса.');
  return [
    base+' · Ранг I: базовый эффект.',
    base+' · Ранг II: усиленный эффект.',
    base+' · Ранг III: максимальный эффект.'
  ];
}

<script>
(function(){
  var back=document.getElementById('ppaRenameBackdrop'),
      panel=document.getElementById('ppaRenamePanel'),
      input=document.getElementById('ppaRenameInput'),
      hint=document.getElementById('ppaRenameHint'),
      cur=document.getElementById('ppaRenameCurrentName');

  function currentName(){
    try{return String((typeof INV!=='undefined'&&INV.playerName)||window.PPA_PLAYER_NAME||'Игрок').trim()||'Игрок'}catch(_){return 'Игрок'}
  }
  function validate(v){
    v=String(v||'').trim();
    if(v.length<3||v.length>18)return 'Ник должен содержать 3–18 символов';
    if(!/^[A-Za-zА-Яа-яЁё0-9_]+$/u.test(v))return 'Разрешены только буквы, цифры и _';
    if(v.toLocaleLowerCase()===currentName().toLocaleLowerCase())return 'Это уже ваш текущий ник';
    return '';
  }
  function positionForKeyboard(){
    if(!window.visualViewport)return;
    try{
      back.style.height=Math.max(120,window.visualViewport.height)+'px';
      back.style.transform='translateY('+Math.max(0,window.visualViewport.offsetTop)+'px)';
    }catch(_){}
  }
  window.openRenameModal=function(){
    if(typeof INV!=='undefined'&&(INV.renameCards||0)<=0)return;
    try{if(typeof closeInventory==='function')closeInventory()}catch(_){}
    cur.textContent=currentName();
    input.value='';
    hint.textContent='Кириллица, латиница, цифры и _';
    hint.classList.remove('bad');
    back.classList.add('on');back.setAttribute('aria-hidden','false');
    positionForKeyboard();
    setTimeout(function(){try{input.focus()}catch(_){}},80);
  };
  window.closeRenameModal=function(){
    back.classList.remove('on');back.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');
    try{input.blur()}catch(_){}
    back.style.height='';back.style.transform='';
  };
  input.addEventListener('input',function(){
    var e=validate(input.value);
    hint.textContent=e||'Ник подходит ✓';
    hint.classList.toggle('bad',!!e);
  });
  document.getElementById('ppaRenameCancel').addEventListener('click',window.closeRenameModal);
  document.getElementById('ppaRenameConfirm').addEventListener('click',async function(){
    var btn=this;
    var n=String(input.value||'').trim(),e=validate(n);
    if(e){hint.textContent=e;hint.classList.add('bad');return}
    if(typeof INV==='undefined'||(INV.renameCards||0)<=0){
      hint.text
